\texttt{Trial \#1: New plan: I failed because I repeatedly tried to take items (cauldron, wands, ingredients) that were not present in the current room, and I ignored the fact that the necessary objects might be in other locations or require specific interactions. I also misinterpreted the game's response to "look at" commands as errors when they were simply unimplemented features. The correct strategy is to first identify the location of every ingredient and tool listed in the recipe by systematically checking all visible objects and moving to adjacent rooms (like the reading room) if needed. Only after confirming the presence of the cauldron, wands, and ingredients should I attempt to take them. I must also strictly follow the recipe steps in order: take the cauldron, then sequentially take each ingredient, then use the wands, and finally add the last ingredient. I will stop attempting to take non{-}existent items immediately and focus on navigation and inventory management.} \\
\\
